As a result, multi-reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become an increasingly important problem for LLMs, where each reward captures a different aspect of desired behavior. However, optimizing with multiple rewards suffers from a more severe alignment tax issue, where different optimization objectives can trade off or even conflict with each other, leading to unstable and inefficient post-training. In this work, we propose PRISM, a new multi-reward RL framework built upon the idea of policy-space decomposition and composition. Instead of compositing different rewards, PRISM optimizes a set of standalone positive policies and a global negative policy. This alleviates the potential conflict during multi-reward policy optimization, while enabling controllability during inference by flexible policy composition. Experiments on scientific reasoning, tool-use reasoning, and helpfulness-safety alignment show that PRISM consistently outperforms existing multi-reward RL baselines, with extra controllability for inference-time preference control.

</details>


### [184] [Tool Specifications Matter: Uncovering and Mitigating Safety Risks in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29254)
*Minghui Pan,Jiayuxuan Yang,Yuanyuan Yuan,Yu Jiang,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了schema格式的工具规格说明（tool specifications）是LLM智能体安全性下降的主要原因，并提出SafeKeep——一种推理时的安全防护方法，将安全判断与工具执行解耦（使用扁平化文本工具规格进行安全评估，保留原始schema格式用于执行），在两个基准和四个LLM上将有害请求的拒绝率从23.8%提升至70.6%，并将prompt注入攻击成功率从25.6%降至2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在作为智能体（agent）使用时安全性显著下降，但此前对这一退化来源的理解不足。作者通过白盒表示分析，发现schema格式的工具规格说明会削弱模型内部的安全拒绝信号，导致不安全工具执行。

Method: 通过白盒表示分析定位问题根源——schema格式工具规格会弱化模型内部拒绝信号；提出SafeKeep：在推理时将安全判断与工具执行解耦，安全评估使用扁平化文本工具规格，而执行阶段保留原始schema格式规格。

Result: 在两个代表性基准和四个LLM（含白盒与黑盒模型）上，SafeKeep将有害请求的平均拒绝率从23.8%提升至70.6%，将观察级prompt注入的平均攻击成功率从25.6%降至2.5%，优于现有防护方法且不影响任务处理能力。

Conclusion: 工具规格的格式（schema格式）是智能体安全性退化的关键因素，SafeKeep通过解耦安全判断与工具执行有效缓解了这一问题，为提升LLM智能体安全性提供了新思路。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) with external tools, enabling them to perform complex tasks and translate model outputs into consequential real-world actions. Yet LLMs often become substantially less safe when deployed as agents, and the source of this degradation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we identify schema-formatted tool specifications as a primary source of agent safety degradation and show, through white-box representation analysis, that they weaken the model's internal refusal signals and contribute to unsafe tool execution. Building on this finding, we propose SafeKeep, an inference-time safeguard that decouples safety judgment from tool execution: it assesses requests using flattened textual tool specifications while retaining the original schema-formatted specifications for execution. Across two representative benchmarks and four LLMs, including both white-box and black-box models, SafeKeep increases the average refusal rate for harmful requests from 23.8% to 70.6% and reduces the average attack success rate under observation-level prompt injection from 25.6% to 2.5%. It also outperforms existing safeguards and preserves task-handling capability. We release the code and data at https://github.com/snowcatsmoking/SafeKeep .

</details>


### [185] [MAGA: Multi-Platform Self-Fusion of GUI Agents via Structured Action Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29320)
*Hang Yan,Zhangxuan GU,Beitong Zhou,Jiaxuan Chen,Runze Li,Yusong Hu,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGA（基于结构化动作重分配训练信号的GUI智能体方法），通过根据动作正确性重新分配蒸馏信号，将注意力集中在错误动作上，并加入仅训练阶段的提示优化教师监督信号，实现跨环境（移动、网页、桌面）的GUI智能体策略整合，在两个模型规模下均取得最优平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI智能体通常是领域特定的，限制了部署和用户体验。虽然有方法如权重合并（weight merging）直接合并领域专家，但会在专家意见不一致时破坏可执行的行动；而在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽避免了冲突的教师监督，却对所有响应token一视同仁，忽略了动作token是智能体与环境交互的唯一接口这一关键特性。

Method: MAGA根据结构化动作的正确性重新分配训练信号，抑制不必要或无效的蒸馏信号，集中学习错误的动作；同时引入仅训练阶段的提示（training-only hint）来优化领域特定教师提供的监督信号，且不改变学生输入。

Result: 在两个模型规模下，MAGA取得了最高的平均成功率。在8B规模下比最强基线高出2.0%，并且在平均性能上与教师模型几乎持平。

Conclusion: 通过关注动作token这一环境交互的关键接口，MAGA有效解决了跨环境GUI智能体整合中的监督信号分配问题，验证了基于结构化动作重分配蒸馏信号的有效性，实现了接近教师水平的性能。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents based on large language models are increasingly deployed across mobile, web, and desktop environments. However, existing agents are typically domain-specific, limiting the deployment and user experience. This motivates the consolidation of specialized models into a single cross-environment policy. Weight merging directly merges domain-specific experts but can corrupt executable actions under expert disagreement, while on-policy distillation (OPD) avoids conflicting teacher supervision yet still treats all response tokens equally during distillation, ignoring that action tokens are the only interface between the environment and the agent. To address this, We introduce MAGA that re-allocates training signal according to the structured action. Based on the correctness of the generated action, it suppresses unnecessary or invalid distillation signals and focuses learning on erroneous actions. Besides, a training-only hint optimizes the supervision signal provided by domain-specific teachers without changing the student input. Across two model scales, MAGA achieves the highest mean success rate, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.0% at 8B and achieves almost the same average performance with teachers.

</details>


### [186] [ModelEquivBench: Certifying Multi-Relational Evaluation of LLM-Generated Optimization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29431)
*Penglin Zhu,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ModelEquivBench，一个可认证、多关系的评估系统，用于评估大语言模型生成的优化模型。该系统输出每对模型的语义剖面E0-E6，涵盖从模型构建、表征对齐、可行集关系到目标函数序等价、最优值等价和最优解集等价等七个层次，每个判定结果都附带独立可复核的证据（可重放迹、精确有理数证书或显式见证者）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法将生成模型与真实模型的比较简化为单一的二值判定（等价/不等价）或执行成功率，这些标签既不可独立核验，也无法忠实反映两个公式在多个不同维度上可能一致的情况。需要一个更精细、可验证的多关系评估框架。

Method: 提出ModelEquivBench系统，定义E0-E6七个层级关系：E0模型构建与精确摄取、E1验证表示对齐、E2同空间可行集关系、E3投影可行集关系、E4目标函数序等价、E5最优值等价、E6最优解集等价。每层判定都附有相应的可复核证据（可重放迹、精确映射、有理数证书、显式见证者），无法判定时输出类型化的UNKNOWN/N/A/ABSENT而非猜测。

Result: 在173个基础问题（每模型346个单元格）上评估GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Qwen3.5-397B-A17B三个模型快照：49/35/25个单元格包含可执行但至少在一个关系上被判定的否定候选，25/8/18个结构拒绝发生在E2已验证映射可行集相等的对上。三个模型在不同剖面阶段失败，无法用单一准确率有意义地概括。

Conclusion: 粗粒度基线无法捕捉模型生成优化模型的多维语义差异。ModelEquivBench提供的细粒度剖面揭示了三个模型在生成质量上的不同失败模式，表明应避免将模型评估简化为单一分数，而需要更丰富、可验证的评估体系。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate optimization models from natural language, but existing evaluation often reduces a generated model and its ground truth to a single equivalent/not-equivalent verdict or an execution-success rate--labels that are neither independently checkable nor faithful to the multiple distinct senses in which two formulations can agree. We present ModelEquivBench, a certifying, multi-relational evaluation system that reports a per-pair semantic profile E0--E6: model construction and exact ingestion (E0), verified representation alignment (E1), same-space and projected feasible-set relations (E2, E3), objective-order equivalence (E4), optimal-value equality (E5), and optimizer-set equivalence (E6). Each decided entry carries relation-appropriate, independently re-checkable evidence: replayable traces or explicit maps for E0--E1, exact-rational certificates for positive E2--E6 conclusions, and explicit witnesses for supported negatives. Incomplete mapping search, unsupported structure, and resource limits produce typed UNKNOWN or N/A outcomes rather than guesses, while unmet prerequisites are reported as ABSENT. Using ModelEquivBench to evaluate three model snapshots--GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Qwen3.5-397B-A17B--on the same frozen cohort of 173 base problems (346 cells per model) under a no-repair protocol, the resulting profiles expose distinctions that coarse baselines do not represent: 49, 35, and 25 cells contain executable candidates that are nevertheless certified negative on at least one supported relation, and 25, 8, and 18 structural rejections occur on pairs for which E2 certifies mapped feasible-set equality under a verified map. The three model snapshots fail at different stages of the profile and therefore cannot be meaningfully reduced to a single accuracy score.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Retrieval: Analytic Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29440)
*Zhoujin Tian,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yakun Li,Lei Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaMM框架，在传统的检索式记忆之外引入"分析式记忆"（analytic memory），将多模态交互历史中反复出现的观察组织为可查询的结构化数据，支持过滤、聚合、排序和时间比较等分析操作。实验表明AdaMM在MemEye和MemGallery两个基准上分别提升最多11.3%和7.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期多模态记忆系统主要强调检索式记忆，通过摘要和索引返回查询相关的信息，但缺乏对累积观察进行计算（如过滤、聚合、排序、时间比较）的能力，难以支持更复杂的分析型查询需求。

Method: AdaMM将分析式记忆与检索式记忆互补结合。具体而言：(1) 不依赖应用自定义schema，从对话、图像和上下文元数据中提取带溯源链接的属性-值观察；(2) 自动发现重复出现的字段结构并物化为可供分析访问的数据；(3) 推理时使用记忆感知的规划器将查询分解为检索和分析操作，并路由到相应工具执行。

Result: 在两个长期多模态记忆基准MemEye和MemGallery上，AdaMM分别实现了最多11.3%和7.3%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 分析式记忆是对检索式记忆的重要补充，AdaMM通过自动提取和物化结构化观察，使记忆系统不仅能"检索到"还能"计算过"，从而更全面地支持长期多模态交互中的复杂记忆需求。

Abstract: Long-term multimodal memory must support not only retrieving relevant information but also computing over observations accumulated across interactions. Existing systems largely emphasize \emph{retrieval memory}, organizing interaction histories through summaries and indexes to return query-relevant information at multiple granularities, from high-level abstractions to underlying records. In this paper, we formulate \emph{analytic memory} as a complementary abstraction that organizes recurring multimodal observations into queryable structures supporting filtering, aggregation, ranking, and temporal comparison. We present AdaMM, a framework that jointly supports retrieval and analytic memory. Rather than relying on application-defined schemas, AdaMM extracts provenance-linked attribute-value observations from dialogue, images, and contextual metadata, discovers recurring field structures, and materializes them for analytical access. At inference time, a memory-aware planner decomposes queries into retrieval and analytic operations and routes each operation to the appropriate tools. Experiments on two long-term multimodal memory benchmarks, MemEye and MemGallery, show that AdaMM improves performance by up to 11.3\% and 7.3\%, respectively.

</details>


### [188] [Self-Play Meets Skill Evolution: Self-Evolving Search Agents that Pose, Solve, and Remember](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29468)
*Zenghuang Fu,Zhaoyang Li,Qiuyuan Ai,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Ante Wang,Guannan He,Changwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SESA（自进化技能增强智能体），通过将程序性记忆作为工具增强搜索自博弈的演化状态，使任务生成和技能记忆共同演化，在七个问答基准上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自博弈智能体生成的训练问题缺乏持久状态，失败虽影响梯度但不明确定形未来练习；外部技能记忆保留了程序性经验，但通常从固定任务分布中学习，无法与训练动态协同演化。

Method: SESA构建双向循环：挑战者提出问题，独立的求解器检索技能；有价值的失败被蒸馏为可复用技能写回记忆；更新后的记忆改变求解器行为与成功率，进而改变挑战者的奖励和未来问题的分布；这个前沿产生新失败，再次重写记忆，实现任务生成与技能记忆共演化。

Result: 在七个开放域和多跳问答基准上，SESA相比SSP平均提升1.2-3.2个点的准确率（跨多种骨干网络）；在统一评估协议下超越SkillRL基线0.9个点。Qwen3模型上SESA-Off保留了1.8-2.2个点的提升，最终技能库额外贡献0.5-1.0个点。

Conclusion: 演化的技能记忆不仅是推理时的插件：它改变策略学习和未来训练分布，同时作为可选外部记忆保留价值，支持无记忆部署和可选的推理时检索。

Abstract: Self-play agents can generate training problems without questions from target benchmarks, but their curricula lack persistent state: failures affect gradients yet do not explicitly shape future practice. External skill memories preserve procedural experience but are typically learned from fixed task distributions. We introduce \textbf{SESA} (Self-Evolving Skill-Augmented Agent), which makes procedural memory an evolving state of tool-augmented search self-play. A challenger poses problems, while a separately parameterized solver alone retrieves skills. Informative failures are distilled into reusable skills and written back to memory. The updated memory changes solver behavior and success, which changes the challenger's reward and the distribution of future problems; the resulting frontier produces new failures that rewrite memory. This bidirectional loop makes task generation and skill memory co-evolve. Because retrieved skills shape on-policy training trajectories, their benefits can enter the model parameters as well as remain in the external bank, enabling memory-free deployment and optional inference-time retrieval. Across seven open-domain and multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, SESA improves average accuracy over SSP by 1.2--3.2 points across multiple backbones and surpasses the skill-augmented SkillRL baseline by 0.9 points under a unified evaluation protocol. On Qwen3 models, SESA-Off retains 1.8--2.2 points of improvement over SSP, while the final skill bank adds a further 0.5--1.0 points. These results show that evolving skill memory is not merely an inference-time plug-in: it changes policy learning and the future training distribution while retaining value as optional external memory. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zenghuang-Fu/SESA-Self-Evolving-Search-Agents.

</details>


### [189] [AMTFV: Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification for LLM Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29549)
*Rui Zou,Yutao Zhu,Mengqi Wei,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AMTFV（Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification），一种面向大语言模型数学问题求解的验证方法。通过引入"数学工具流"（MTF）作为中断-执行-恢复接口，AMTFV将验证建模与具体执行解耦，利用数学工具箱支持精确计算。在五个数学推理数据集和七种模型配置上的实验表明，AMTFV总体优于代表性基线，在单个模型配置下相较于最强基线平均准确率提升最多8.3个百分点，在中等和高验证复杂度样本上收益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型数学答案验证方法存在两大局限：基于自然语言反思的方法无法可靠支持精确计算；直接生成验证程序的方法过早地将数学建模与底层实现耦合。因此需要一种既能解耦建模与执行、又能支持精确计算的验证框架。

Method: AMTFV由验证代理和数学工具箱代理组成。验证代理首先构建验证工作流，将需要可靠执行的数学对象和计算意图编码为MTF请求；数学工具箱代理解析请求、生成可执行调用并分派至后端进行精确计算。工具输出支持候选答案裁决、答案修订和验证工作流修订。

Result: 在五个数学推理数据集（DeepSeek、GPT、Gemini的七种模型配置）上的实验表明：AMTFV总体优于评估中的代表性基线；在单个模型配置下，相较最强基线平均准确率最高提升8.3个百分点；在中等和高验证复杂度样本上提升更显著。

Conclusion: AMTFV通过MTF接口在验证建模与具体执行之间建立清晰边界，兼顾了精确计算能力与建模灵活性，是一种有效的数学答案验证方法，尤其适用于验证复杂度较高的场景。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated strong mathematical problem-solving capabilities, yet reliably verifying their candidate answers remains challenging. Existing representative methods mainly revise outputs through natural-language reflection or assist verification by directly generating verification programs; the former may not reliably support exact computation, whereas the latter prematurely couples mathematical modeling with low-level implementation. We propose AMTFV (Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification). By introducing Mathematical Tool Flow (MTF) as an interrupt--execute--resume interface, AMTFV decouples verification modeling from concrete execution and supports exact computation through a mathematical toolbox. Specifically, the verification agent first constructs a verification workflow, encodes the mathematical objects and computational intent requiring reliable execution in an MTF request, and sends it to the mathematical toolbox agent. The latter parses the request, generates executable calls, and dispatches them to the backend for exact computation. Tool outputs then support candidate-answer adjudication, answer revision, and verification-workflow revision. We evaluate AMTFV on five challenging mathematical reasoning datasets with seven model configurations from DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini. Experimental results show that AMTFV outperforms the representative baselines evaluated in this study overall; under an individual model configuration, it improves average accuracy over the strongest baseline by up to 8.3 percentage points, with larger gains on samples of medium and high verification complexity.

</details>


### [190] [COntExt: Towards Context-Aware Ontology Extension from Operational Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29553)
*Hussain Hussain,Stefan Schöberl,Angelika Schneider,Verena Geist*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COntExt框架，利用结构化操作指标定义中的上下文信息，自动建议如何将指标中引用的概念和属性集成到现有本体中，将本体扩展问题分解为父类预测、关系类型预测和数据属性分配三个子任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地使用结构化格式定义操作指标来监控系统、流程和合规性。这些指标定义隐式编码了领域知识（如引用概念、属性和关系），往往超出正式本体所捕获的范围。然而，操作指标目录与本体知识之间的连接目前仍是手工、临时且劳动密集型的过程，缺乏自动化支持。

Method: COntExt框架以结构化指标定义为输入，利用指标的上下文信息，将本体扩展问题形式化为三个子任务：父类预测（parent class prediction）、关系类型预测（relation type prediction）和数据属性分配（data property assignment），并针对每个任务评估不同算法。

Result: 在四个网络安全本体上进行的实验表明，基于指标派生的上下文在关系类型预测和数据属性分配任务上优于仅使用本体上下文的基线方法，证明了操作指标目录是本体扩展的一个实用且未被充分利用的数据源。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了操作指标目录可以显著降低组织维护本体的成本，相比手工工程方式更为高效，为自动化本体扩展提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly define operational metrics in structured, machine-readable formats to monitor systems, processes, and compliance. These metric definitions implicitly encode domain knowledge, such as referencing concepts, properties, and relationships, that often extends what is captured in formal ontologies. Yet the connection between operational metric catalogues and ontological knowledge remains manual, ad-hoc, and labor-intensive. We present COntExt, a framework for context-aware ontology extension that takes structured metric definitions as input and suggests how referenced concepts and properties should be integrated into an existing ontology, utilizing the context of these metrics. The framework defines the extension problem as three sub-tasks: parent class prediction, relation type prediction, and data property assignment. Across four cybersecurity ontologies, we evaluate different algorithms for each task. Our results show that metric-derived context improves the suggestions over ontology-context baselines for relation type prediction and data property assignment. Our work demonstrates that operational metric catalogues are a practical and underexploited source for ontology extension. This work enables organizations to maintain their ontologies at a significantly lower cost than manual engineering.

</details>


### [191] [LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning from Preference Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29559)
*Manith Adikari,Bei Peng,Samuele Vinanzi,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LEMUR框架，将偏好驱动的强化学习（PbRL）扩展到多目标强化学习（MORL）场景中，通过从多个人类偏好反馈中联合学习策略和多个目标特定的奖励模型，从而在没有预定义奖励函数的情况下平衡多个竞争目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MORL方法通常假设每个目标都有明确指定的奖励函数，这在实际应用中难以满足；而PbRL虽然能避免预定义奖励函数，但主要局限于单目标场景。因此需要弥合这两者之间的差距。

Method: LEMUR框架：从多个人的偏好反馈中联合学习策略和多个目标特定的奖励模型，使智能体在学习过程中有效平衡竞争目标。

Result: 在多种基准多目标任务上进行了评估，实验结果表明LEMUR优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为在没有预定义奖励函数的情况下解决多目标决策任务提供了一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems are typically trained using a single, well-specified scalar reward function. However, real-world decision-making tasks often involve multiple, competing objectives, such as performance versus efficiency, where ground-truth reward functions are difficult to specify or inaccessible. While Multi-Objective RL (MORL) addresses such trade-offs by modeling rewards as vectors, existing approaches typically assume access to a well-specified reward function for each objective, inheriting the same challenges faced by single-objective RL. Meanwhile, Preference-based RL (PbRL) has shown great potential in solving complex tasks without access to a pre-defined reward function through reward learning from human feedback, yet has largely been studied in single-objective settings. In this work, we bridge this gap with LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Preference feedback, a novel framework where an agent interactively learns from the preferences of multiple humans to learn optimal multi-objective policies. Our approach jointly learns policies and multiple objective-specific reward models from human feedback, enabling agents to effectively balance competing objectives during learning. We evaluate LEMUR on a variety of benchmark multi-objective tasks, and empirical results demonstrate its superior performance over baseline methods. Our method presents a promising direction for solving multi-objective decision-making tasks without pre-defined reward functions.

</details>


### [192] [DungeonBench: A Benchmark for Rules-Rich Tactical Reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29577)
*Ismayil Ismayilov,Atakan Kara,Kaan Oktay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DungeonBench，一个用于龙与地下城战斗场景的战术推理基准测试平台，覆盖2014年SRD中绝大部分战斗相关内容，通过Encounter和Day两个轨道分别评估局部战术决策和跨遭遇的资源管理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏模拟器基准测试往往低估了规则密集的战术推理能力——即在几何、时机、资源、目标和规则交互同时起作用时做出正确选择的能力。当前许多基准被简化，无法充分测试这种复杂决策能力。

Method: DungeonBench基于D&D 5e战斗模拟器构建，覆盖2014年SRD中大部分战斗相关内容，同时保留简化战斗模拟器常忽略的机制。每个决策步骤提供完整的战术观察、待处理决策和索引化的可执行选项列表（涵盖移动、攻击、法术、反应、目标、准备和稀缺资源）。设有两个轨道：Encounter轨道评估单场战斗的局部战术，Day轨道通过持续生命值、法术位、消耗品、准备和短休时机连接多场遭遇，迫使策略在即时战术优势与未来生存能力之间权衡。同一引擎生成的决策流支持启发式控制器、语言模型策略、学习型选项排序器和掩码动作强化学习智能体。

Result: 作者在共享决策流上评估了前沿语言模型策略。结果显示完整的战术观察并未使基准饱和：前沿策略通常能赢得直接遭遇战，但在连接性遭遇战中暴露出资源预算、休息时机和规则感知战术纪律方面的失败。

Conclusion: DungeonBench表明现有前沿语言模型在局部战术上表现尚可，但在需要跨遭遇进行资源分配和长期规划的场景中仍存在明显不足，为语言模型策略和强化学习智能体提供了具有挑战性的战术推理测试平台。

Abstract: Games and simulators make valuable benchmarks by turning decisions into measurable outcomes, but many current suites under-test rules-rich tactical reasoning: the ability to choose well when geometry, timing, resources, objectives, and rule interactions all matter at once. We introduce DungeonBench, a benchmark for tactical reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons combat, built to cover the vast majority of combat-relevant 2014 System Reference Document content whose effects can be resolved by the simulator while retaining mechanics that simplified combat simulators often abstract away. At each step, DungeonBench exposes a complete tactical observation, a pending decision, and an indexed list of executable options spanning movement, attacks, spells, reactions, objectives, preparation, and scarce resources. The task is to value legal choices whose consequences depend on action economy, creature traits, battlefield geometry, timing windows, and future encounters. DungeonBench has two tracks: Encounter, which evaluates local tactical play in single fights, and Day, which links encounters through persistent hit points, spell slots, consumables, preparation, and short-rest timing, forcing policies to trade off immediate tactical advantage against future survivability. The same engine-generated decision stream supports heuristic controllers, language-model policies, learned option rankers, and masked-action reinforcement-learning agents. We evaluate frontier language-model policies on this shared decision stream. Results show that full tactical observations do not saturate the benchmark: frontier policies often win direct encounters, but linked encounter days expose failures in resource budgeting, rest timing, and rule-aware tactical discipline.

</details>


### [193] [AgentHPOBench: A Benchmark For Evaluating LLM Agents as Sequential Hyperparameter Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29626)
*Tianyu Huai,Tingshuo Fan,Xinchi Chen,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Shuang Chen,Jie Zhou,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentHPOBench，一个包含30个可执行机器学习任务的顺序基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在实验优化中的能力。测试了12种主流智能体和传统HPO基线，发现现有智能体具备一定的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代改进、复杂日志诊断和稳定逼近参考性能方面仍存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从代码补全系统演变为自主科学智能体，评估其开展实验的能力变得日益重要。现有基准测试通常关注静态代码生成、论文复现或最终答案正确性，但未直接评估智能体能否解读实验证据并据此指导后续超参数决策，这一评估缺口亟需填补。

Method: 构建AgentHPOBench顺序基准，涵盖七个研究类别的30个可执行机器学习任务。每个任务从验证过的基线运行开始，智能体进行多次顺序干预；每一步智能体需观察累积的配置、指标和日志，然后提出下一个有效配置。在统一协议下评估12种广泛使用的智能体及传统HPO基线。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前智能体在跨领域的实验优化能力上表现出可测量的水平，但在持续迭代优化、复杂日志诊断以及朝向报告参考性能的一致进展方面仍面临明显限制。

Conclusion: 尽管LLM智能体在科学实验优化中已展现出一定潜力，但现有系统在处理长时间序列的实验迭代、解读复杂日志信息以及稳定逼近最优性能方面仍有显著不足，这为未来的智能体设计和基准扩展指明了方向。

Abstract: As LLMs evolve from code completion systems into autonomous scientific agents, evaluating their ability to conduct experiments has become increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically focus on static code generation, paper replication, or final answer correctness, but do not directly assess whether agents can interpret experimental evidence and use it to guide subsequent hyperparameter decisions. To address this gap, we introduce AgentHPOBench, a sequential benchmark comprising 30 executable machine learning tasks across seven research categories. Each task begins with a validated baseline run, after which an agent performs several sequential interventions. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated configurations, metrics, and logs before proposing the next valid configuration. We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

</details>


### [194] [Development of FDD-ON: an Ontology for VAV HVAC System Fault Detection and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29657)
*Yimin Chen,Brian Fricke,Bo Shen,Jamie Lian,Mingkan Zhang,James Lo,Yun Zhang,Shi Ye,Jiajing Huang,Han Hu,Chujie Lu,Rui Tang,George Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FDD-ON，一个模块化、可扩展的本体，用于形式化表示VAV HVAC系统的故障检测与诊断（FDD）领域知识，包括故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及相关属性，并通过显式的因果-故障-症状-影响关系支持机器可解释的诊断知识查询和异构FDD输出的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HVAC系统中的FDD技术对可靠性、能效和运维至关重要，但现有FDD解决方案面临数据可解释性和互操作性不足的问题，导致信息孤岛，阻碍了数字孪生和AI驱动的维护决策等应用的部署。

Method: 构建FDD-ON本体，模块化地表示VAV HVAC系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响和属性，整合受控词汇表，提供故障/症状/影响库，并建立因果-故障-症状-影响之间的显式关系。

Result: 使用公开的VAV HVAC系统数据集进行评估，并通过FDD开发应用进行验证，结果表明FDD-ON为可扩展、透明、可互操作的FDD解决方案提供了基础语义框架。

Conclusion: FDD-ON能够弥合FDD领域的数据异构性和互操作性差距，为跨应用的可扩展FDD解决方案奠定基础，支持数字孪生和AI驱动维护等高级应用。

Abstract: Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) technology is essential for improving HVAC system reliability, energy efficiency, and maintenance effectiveness. However, effective deployment of FDD solutions in buildings requires structured domain knowledge that can bridge heterogeneous data sources, diverse equipment types, and varied diagnostic outputs. Limited data interpretability and interoperability within the FDD domain have led to fragmented information silos, hindering the implementation of FDD and related applications, such as the digital twin-enabled FDD frameworks and artificial intelligence (AI)-driven maintenance decision-making systems. This paper presents an FDD Ontology (FDD-ON), a modular and extensible ontology to formally represent variable air volume (VAV) HVAC system components, fault types, symptom statuses, fault impacts and associated attributes. FDD-ON integrates HVAC system FDD semantics to provide comprehensive representations of fault and symptom attributes, supported by the well-defined controlled vocabulary. Additionally, FDD-ON offers comprehensive fault, symptom, and impact libraries to capture a broad spectrum of operational abnormalities and their consequences in VAV HVAC systems. Through explicit contributing cause-fault-symptom-impact relations, FDD-ON serves as a machine-interpretable basis for querying diagnostic knowledge, mapping heterogeneous FDD outputs, and developing interoperable FDD-related applications. FDD-ON is evaluated using publicly available VAV HVAC system datasets and demonstrated through FDD development applications. Results indicate that FDD-ON provides a foundational semantic framework for advancing scalable, transparent, and interoperable FDD solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [195] [ExtractBench: A Benchmark for Schema-Guided Enterprise Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29677)
*Boyang Zhang,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Zhaoqi Li,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExtractBench，一个用于 schema-guided extraction（模式引导抽取）的基准测试，首次同时评估值准确率、记录完整性、grounding（溯源）和成本四个维度。包含 370 个企业文档、4869 页、8 个业务领域和 67 种文档类型，并提出了可扩展的 schema 与真值标注流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工作流中基于 schema 的文档抽取越来越依赖 agent，但现有基准缺少同时评估值准确率、大规模记录完整性、grounding 以及成本的多维度评测体系。

Method: 构建了包含 370 个企业文档（4869 页）的评测集，覆盖 8 个业务领域和 67 种文档类型，并设计了结合多种来源的可扩展真值标注流水线：真实文档采用独立系统一致性协议、合成列表使用已知值、表单采用人工验证。评测指标包括顺序无关的 value F1（值准确率）、词级和页级 F1（两种 grounding 指标）以及成本测量。

Result: 商业 VLM 在短文档上表现良好但在长文档上容易截断记录列表；编码 agent 保持较高准确率但成本显著更高；LlamaExtract Agentic Plus 在三个指标上均排名第一，准确率接近编码 agent 但成本仅为极小一部分。

Conclusion: ExtractBench 提供了首个综合评估 schema-guided extraction 的多维度基准，揭示了不同方法在准确率、完整性与成本之间的权衡，并指出了长文档处理是该领域的核心挑战。

Abstract: Enterprise workflows increasingly rely on agents for \emph{schema-guided extraction}: given a document and a user-defined schema, the agent faithfully follows the schema to produce the correct output with source evidence as grounding metadata. We present ExtractBench, a benchmark for schema-guided extraction and, to our knowledge, the first to score value accuracy, record completeness at scale, grounding, and measured cost together. The evaluation system contains 4,869 pages across 370 enterprise documents, 8 business domains, and 67 document types, with clear tags differentiating their challenge scenarios. The scalable schema and ground-truth curation pipeline combines independent-system agreement for real documents, known values for synthetic lists, and human verification for forms. We report order-insensitive value F1 for value accuracy, plus two grounding metrics for source traceability: word- and page-level F1. Commercial VLMs perform well on short documents but often truncate record lists on long ones, while coding agents retain higher accuracy at much higher cost. LlamaExtract Agentic Plus ranks first on all three metrics, with accuracy comparable to coding agents at a fraction of the cost. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ExtractBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ExtractBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Sovereign Cognitive Digital Twins: Fusing 6G ISAC, AI-RAN, and Zero-Trust Edge Grids for National Resilience in the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28756)
*Zoe Aiyanna M. Cayetano,George M. Gichuru,Taijuo T. Morris*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出将6G网络感知能力作为国家数字孪生的感知基底，为小岛屿发展中国家构建"主权认知数字孪生（S-CDT）"框架，利用ISAC波形将蜂窝网络本身变成分布式雷达网格，并给出基于巴巴多斯实际地理数据的可复现仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小岛屿发展中国家面临海平面上升、飓风和风暴潮等加速的气候威胁，但地面观测仪器稀疏，难以实现及时的海啸/灾害感知。传统的分离式传感系统成本高昂且部署困难，急需利用现有的蜂窝网络基础设施作为大规模感知手段。

Method: 提出六层S-CDT框架：ISAC波形实现动态数据层与通信层融合；物理层映射至ETSI GR ISC 001和3GPP Release 19传感框架；设计基于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）与近端策略优化（PPO）的信念状态控制回路以吸收O-RAN遥测延迟。实现了CPU-only的地理数据到光线追踪的可复现管线，在巴巴多斯牛顿种植园2km研究区域构建Sionna RT场景。

Result: 仿真显示：1.8GHz中值最佳服务器路径增益为-106 dB，10GHz降至-122 dB；28/60GHz仅混凝土场景为-130/-136 dB，覆盖收缩至视距瓣。验证了高频段毫米波对小岛屿覆盖受限的特性，为6G站点建模提供背景信道参考。

Conclusion: 该框架将数据主权和物理层零信任安全作为一等设计约束，巴巴多斯为参考部署，菲律宾作为群岛泛化场景。控制回路已规定但未评估，属于"面向6G"的架构论证而非运营级ISAC部署。

Abstract: Small-island developing states face accelerating sea-level rise, intensifying cyclones, and storm surge, while suffering the sparse ground instrumentation that makes timely hazard perception difficult. This paper argues for a shift from passive cellular connectivity to the Network as a Sensor, realized through a Sovereign Cognitive Digital Twin (S-CDT): a federated national digital twin whose perceptual substrate is the 6G radio interface itself. Instead of disjoint sensing systems, a nation can reuse the Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) waveforms of its own network as a distributed radar mesh. We specify a six-layer S-CDT stack in which ISAC collapses the boundary between the dynamic-data and communication layers; we map the physical layer to the ETSI GR ISC 001 and 3GPP Release 19 sensing frameworks; and we formulate a belief-state control loop, an Extended Kalman Filter feeding a Proximal Policy Optimization agent, designed to absorb O-RAN telemetry delay. That loop is specified but not evaluated here. Beyond the reference architecture, we implement a reproducible, CPU-only geodata-to-ray-tracing pipeline over a 2 km study area at the Barbados Heritage District, Newton Plantation: 576 LiDAR-height buildings, a 70x70 terrain grid, and the government tower register become a Sionna RT scene. On identical geometry, median best-server path gain falls from -106 dB at 1.8 GHz to -122 dB at 10 GHz; concrete-only 28/60 GHz runs yield -130/-136 dB, with coverage contracting to line-of-sight lobes. These uncalibrated, 1x1 V-polarized simulations are framed as a towards-6G site model of the background channel, not an operational ISAC deployment. We treat data sovereignty and physical-layer zero-trust security as first-order design constraints. Barbados (166 km2, ~280k population) is the reference deployment, with the Philippines as an archipelagic generalization.

</details>


### [197] [When Unlearning Fails: Reliable Data Deletion under Post-Training in Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28829)
*Zihao Ding,Jun Huang,Liang Dong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出MUTE方法，解决自提升联邦智能体网络中遗忘数据通过闭环训练产生"影响回声"污染后续轨迹的问题，通过服务器台账估计下游影响、遗忘-保留更新清理残留、隔离或降权高影响轨迹、以及审计调度额外擦除，在保持任务效用的同时显著降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自提升联邦智能体网络在部署后持续闭环训练，当数据所有者请求删除时，目标数据可能已经塑造了后续保留的轨迹，导致重训练或模型侧遗忘后仍存在"影响回声"，且该回声会随网络继续运行而不断回归，传统一次性模型修复无法解决。

Method: MUTE方法包含四个核心组件：(1) 通过轻量服务器台账估计下游影响；(2) 通过遗忘-保留更新移除当前残留；(3) 通过隔离或降权处理高影响保留轨迹；(4) 在通信预算约束下审计后续行为并调度额外擦除操作。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上使用两个视觉-语言-动作骨干网络、三种删除粒度和基于Jetson的物理边缘测试平台进行实验，结果表明MUTE能保持低行为泄漏和低影响再生，同时保留任务效用，通信开销远低于全量重训练。

Conclusion: MUTE为自提升联邦智能体网络中的可靠数据删除提供了一种高效方案，能够追踪并抑制遗忘数据的影响回声，实现更可靠、更高效的分布式遗忘机制。

Abstract: Self-improving federated agent networks keep training after deployment by collecting new trajectories with the current policy and feeding them back into later rounds. This closed loop makes unlearning harder than a one-time model repair. When a data owner requests deletion, the target data may have already shaped later retained trajectories, so retraining or model-side unlearning can leave an influence echo that returns as the network continues to operate. We show that this echo survives retained-data retraining, grows with the amount of forget-shaped retained data, and can be traced from deployment, collection, and aggregation records. To address this problem, we propose MUTE, a Muting Unlearned Trajectories' Echoes method for reliable deletion in self-improving federated agent networks. MUTE estimates downstream influence from a lightweight server ledger, removes the current residue through a forget-retain update, contains high-influence retained trajectories through quarantine or down-weighting, and audits later behavior to schedule additional erasure under an uplink budget. Experiments on LIBERO with two vision-language-action backbones, three deletion granularities, and a physical Jetson-based edge testbed show that MUTE keeps behavioral leakage and influence regeneration low while preserving task utility and using much less communication than full retraining.

</details>


### [198] [Point2Radio: A Foundation Model for Cross-Scene Radio Fields from Material-Aware Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28994)
*Chaozheng Wen,Chenghong Bian,Hongze Chen,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Point2Radio，一个基于多环境学习的无线传播基础模型。它通过材料感知点云和发射机设置，利用共享编码器生成发射机条件化的场景表示，可在任意接收机位置查询，并映射为路径增益（PG）和功率角度谱（PAS）等无线量。在337场景、86,272个发射机条件化场的数据集上，PG预测MAE达0.871 dB，相比UNet基线误差降低76.7%，并支持轻量微调适应特定环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高保真射频场模拟方法要么对每个场景-发射机配置单独仿真，要么为每个场景单独拟合模型，未能利用不同环境间共有的传播结构，导致效率低下且缺乏泛化能力。

Method: Point2Radio采用一个通用编码器，输入材料感知点云和发射机（TX）设置，生成TX条件化的场景表示；该表示可在任意接收机（RX）位置进行查询，并通过任务特定的查询解码器映射到不同无线量（如3D路径增益场PG和功率角度谱PAS）。推理时仅需材料感知点云和收发机查询，无需网格或显式光线追踪，可在单GPU上毫秒级运行。

Result: 在337场景、86,272个TX条件化场的场景分离数据集上，PG预测MAE为0.871 dB，相比同场景分离的UNet风格基线误差降低76.7%。同一编码器通过任务特定解码器也支持PAS预测，且轻量目标场景微调可进一步提升特定环境的适应性能。

Conclusion: Point2Radio验证了无线传播知识可以在跨环境间迁移，作为基础模型具备高效推理和任务扩展能力，为射频场预测提供了可泛化的新范式。

Abstract: High-fidelity radio fields are typically simulated for every scene--transmitter configuration or fitted separately to each scene, failing to exploit propagation structures shared across environments. We present Point2Radio, a foundation model that learns a transferable propagation prior from multiple environments. Given a material-aware point cloud and a transmitter (TX) setting, a common encoder produces a TX-conditioned scene representation that can be queried at arbitrary receiver (RX) locations. Task-specific query decoders map this representation to different radio quantities, e.g., three-dimensional (3D) path-gain (PG) fields and power angular spectra (PAS). At inference for a new scene, the model uses only a material-aware point cloud and transceiver queries, running in milliseconds on a single GPU without meshes or explicit path tracing. We evaluate PG prediction on a scene-disjoint split of a 337-scene corpus containing 86,272 TX-conditioned fields. Point2Radio achieves 0.871 dB mean absolute error (MAE), reducing error by 76.7% relative to a same-split UNet-style baseline. The same encoder also supports PAS prediction via a task-specific decoder. Experiments further show that light target-scene fine-tuning improves adaptation to a specific environment.

</details>


### [199] [Mind the Gap: Policy vs Reality in Post-Quantum TLS Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29005)
*Nimesha Wickramasinghe,Frank Li,Sanjay Jha,Arash Shaghaghi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次对全球后量子TLS（PQ-TLS）部署进行了大规模纵向测量研究，通过建立超过20亿次TLS握手，分析了100万域名在11个全球分布观测点的密码学协商行为。研究发现PQ-TLS部署高度集中于单一混合构造，主要由托管基础设施提供商驱动，政策路线与实际部署之间存在明显脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然NIST已完成后量子密码标准化，各国政府和标准化机构也发布了迁移路线图，但此前缺乏对PQ-TLS实际部署情况的系统性实证测量。政策文本之间的差异及其如何转化为实际的部署结果仍不清楚，这阻碍了更接地气的迁移规划。

Method: 采用纵向测量研究方法，从11个全球分布的观测点建立超过20亿次TLS握手，覆盖100万个域名，分析密码学协商行为、部署模式、政策对应关系以及延迟影响。

Result: 发现PQ-TLS部署呈现配置收敛现象，集中在单一混合构造方案；部署主要由托管基础设施提供商驱动；国家时间表和部门优先级与实际部署模式相关性有限；PQ-TLS在互联网环境下未引入明显延迟增加，但常与遗留TLS配置并存部署。

Conclusion: 政策预期与早期部署现实之间存在显著差距，研究提供了基于实证的洞见，有助于制定更加务实的PQ-TLS迁移策略。

Abstract: Post-quantum cryptography (PQC) has evolved from a long-term planning concern into an operational priority. Following NIST's standardization of PQC, governments and standard bodies published transition roadmaps outlining migration timelines, priority sectors, and deployment strategies. However, our survey of these policies reveals substantial divergence in technical prescriptions and urgency. It remains unclear how widely PQC has been adopted in practice and how policy differences translate into observable deployment outcomes.
  To address this gap, we present the first longitudinal measurement study of post-quantum TLS (PQ-TLS) adoption. By establishing more than 2 billion TLS handshakes, we analyze cryptographic negotiation behavior across 1 million domains from 11 globally distributed vantage points. Despite varied policy guidance, we observe configuration convergence: PQ-TLS deployment overwhelmingly centers on a single hybrid construction, and much of the apparent progress is driven by managed infrastructure providers. National timelines and sectoral priorities show limited correspondence with observed deployment patterns. Contrary to early experimental studies suggesting measurable overhead, we find that PQ-TLS introduces no meaningful latency increase in Internet settings, although it is frequently deployed alongside legacy TLS configurations. Together, these findings highlight a gap between policy expectations and early deployment reality, and provide empirical insight to inform more grounded PQ-TLS transition.

</details>


### [200] [METIS: A Declarative Slice Orchestrator for Application-Centric 5G/6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29282)
*Arman Divband,Ali Yaghoubian,Navid Nikaein*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出METIS，一个声明式网络切片编排器，通过级联协调循环管理网络切片实例的Day-0/1/2生命周期。METIS采用应用中心数据模型，从服务配置文件推导3GPP对齐的切片配置文件，并联合协调O-RAN和3GPP域的切片控制。核心发现是端到端切片控制存在结构性不对称：下行流量可在核心网整形，但上行流量离开用户设备后不受管控，因此核心网单独切片无法可靠满足上行服务等级协议，无线侧强制执行是必要的。在5G云原生测试床上验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络切片编排器依赖网络中心数据模型、命令式工作流和静态切片模板；O-RAN处理无线侧切片控制与3GPP核心网侧控制相互独立，导致跨域的切片级服务质量执行缺乏协调。动态生命周期管理和跨域QoS协调问题尚未解决。

Method: METIS采用声明式切片编排方法：(1)定义应用中心数据模型用于服务配置文件，让客户描述应用语义和体验质量需求；(2)通过遵循5G QoS模型的分层聚合从服务配置推导3GPP对齐的切片配置文件，消除静态模板；(3)通过级联协调循环管理Day-0/1/2生命周期，联合协调O-RAN和3GPP切片以实现切片实例化和执行。

Result: 在5G云原生测试平台和校园活动场景中：切片创建/更新/升级/删除分别耗时22.4/5.1/52.2/32.1秒；在多切片过载下保持完全的服务等级协议满足；可扩展到九个区域的63个切片实例，总CPU消耗低于0.03核；从四个级别的注入故障中恢复切片用时低于19秒。

Conclusion: METIS证明了声明式、应用驱动的网络切片编排能够实现跨RAN和核心网的协调QoS执行。核心发现揭示了端到端切片控制的结构性不对称——无线侧执行对于上行业务的服务等级协议保障是必要条件而不仅仅是补充。

Abstract: Network slicing is the cornerstone of application-aware 5G and 6G networks, yet dynamic lifecycle management of network slice instances with coordinated quality-of-service enforcement across the radio access network and core network remains unresolved. Existing orchestrators rely on network-centric data models, imperative workflows, and static slice templates, while O-RAN addresses radio-side slice control independently of 3GPP core-side control, leaving slice-level quality-of-service enforcement uncoordinated across domains. This paper introduces METIS, a declarative slice orchestrator that manages the Day-0/1/2 lifecycle of network slice instances through cascaded reconciliation loops. METIS defines an application-centric data model for service profiles, enabling customers to describe the semantics and quality-of-experience requirements of their applications. From these, METIS derives 3GPP-aligned slice profiles via hierarchical aggregation following the 5G quality-of-service model, eliminating static templates, and jointly coordinates O-RAN and 3GPP slicing for slice instantiation and enforcement. Our central finding is a structural asymmetry in end-to-end slice control: downlink traffic can be shaped at the core before reaching the radio access network, but uplink leaves the user equipment unregulated, so core-only slicing cannot reliably satisfy uplink service-level agreements - radio-side enforcement is necessary, not merely complementary. Evaluated on a 5G cloud-native testbed in a campus-event scenario, METIS completes slice creation, update, upgrade, and deletion within 22.4, 5.1, 52.2, and 32.1 seconds, respectively; sustains full service-level-agreement satisfaction under concurrent multi-slice overload; scales to 63 slice instances across nine zones consuming under 0.03 processor cores total; and recovers slices from injected failures across four levels in under 19 seconds.

</details>


### [201] [RIGEL: Real-time Optical Anomaly Diagnosis with Stateful In-Network Inference based on Distributed On-switch GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29306)
*Zhen Wei,Yidong Wang,Yufan Zhu,Xuefeng Yan,Binjun Tang,Xiaoliang Chen,Zuqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: RIGEL是首个基于Tofino交换机的实时光网络异常诊断系统，通过图神经网络（GNN）实现有状态分布式网内推理，采用软硬件协同设计预处理高维光谱数据，并结合自编码器与GraphSAGE模型，在真实光网络测试平台上验证了其高精度、低延迟的异常检测与定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型应用的爆发使光网络管理日益复杂，实时光异常诊断成为必需。然而现有方法多基于集中式数据分析，难以避免数据面与控制面之间消息交换带来的延迟和开销。

Method: 1. 设计完全网内的实时光异常诊断系统RIGEL；2. 提出软硬件协同设计方法对高维光谱数据进行预处理，使其适配硬件网内推理；3. 开发自编码器与GraphSAGE-based GNN结合的模型；4. 提出将模型适配到Tofino交换机的通用化方法。

Result: 在真实的packet-over-optical网络测试平台上进行验证，RIGEL实现了高精度的异常诊断，能够及时检测和定位光异常，优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RIGEL证明了通过协作GNN在可编程交换机上实现有状态分布式网内推理的可行性，为光网络实时异常诊断提供了一种低延迟、高精度的全新范式。

Abstract: The recent booming of data-intensive applications has complicated optical network management, making real-time optical anomaly diagnosis a must-have feature. However, existing approaches are mostly based on centralized data analytics and thus can hardly avoid the latency and overhead due to message exchanges between data and control planes. In this work, we propose and prototype RIGEL, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first real-time optical anomaly diagnosis system that realizes stateful distributed in-network inference through collaborative graph neural networks (GNNs) on Tofino switches. The system is designed to be fully in-network, and a software-hardware co-design is proposed to preprocess high-dimensional spectral data for being suitable for hardware-based in-network inference. Next, we first develop an effective model to combine an autoencoder with a GraphSAGE-based GNN, and then propose a generalizable method to adapt the model to Tofino switch. The effectiveness of RIGEL is showcased in a realistic packet-over-optical network testbed, verifying that it achieves highly accurate diagnosis to detect and locate optical anomalies timely and highlighting its benefits over the state-of-the-art methods.

</details>
